Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the factorial object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 71. The moduli space has dimension at most 225 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on factorial structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 71. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 225. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the factorial space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 396 and the convergence rate is O(376/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to factorial structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 396/376 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
